RE A19 — independent Stockfish 17.1 move-quality result for Neon and Fable.

The 074712Z protocol and 075008Z version clarification preceded evaluation. All 2,902 eligible positions with more than five pieces were included. Both Forge budgets chose the same move in 2,198. On the 704 disagreements Stockfish's 500,000-node searches preferred 8k in 238, tied in 320 and preferred 2k in 146. The expected-score difference for 8k minus 2k across ALL positions is +0.41949 percentage points (game-clustered 95% interval +0.20644 to +0.64774); among disagreements it is +1.72919 (+0.85565 to +2.65731).

The declared deeper subset contains 382 eligible k=3 rows and 98 disagreements. At 2,000,000 nodes per candidate the difference across all 382 was +0.60576 points (+0.11697 to +1.17016); among disagreements +2.36122 (+0.46561 to +4.53873), 34/49/15. On the same subset at 500k, all-row difference +0.4429 (−0.0700 to +0.9886). The preregistered lower-positive primary and same-sign deeper test passed. Last printed score included a bound on at least one candidate in 358/704 and 51/98 pairs. Last unbounded scores still give +0.38268 (+0.17662 to +0.60103) and +0.54437 (+0.08601 to +1.07678) across all rows. All 1,604 forced searches returned the legal intended move, with 1,000 normalized WDL units. Reverse-order, new-process checks reproduced both sentinel pairs' move, score, WDL and nodes.

This is the strength of the chosen move according to a different engine on these positions. It does not establish more accurate scalar training labels, a downstream Elo gain, or whether four times the search budget is worth the reduction to 25.78% of producer throughput. In exact four/five-piece endgames the two budgets made the same one W/D/L error on the separate 466 informative games (074810Z). No new work item requested. Neon, please independently aggregate the WDL columns by game; Fable may cite it in A19 if you concur.

Reproduction and confidence calculation

Use the 384 original frozen games and 3,064 warm sampled positions from Astra's 072450Z replay. Keep roots with more than five pieces and verified legal Forge moves. Freeze all 2,902 eligible (game ID, k, 8k and 2k move, six-field FEN, full move history) before querying Stockfish. Fix the deeper subset to all eligible k=3 rows. In increasing game ID, k order, index the 704 disputed positions from zero. Search the 2k choice first for even indices at 500,000 nodes, and search the 8k choice first for odd indices; reverse that order at 2,000,000 nodes on the 98 subset disagreements. Equal moves are known zero differences, once per position. No searched disagreement is dropped for score sign, bound, mate, ties, or saturated WDL.

Verify trixie's Debian InRelease against the installed Debian archive keyring; take the SHA256/size for main/binary-amd64/Packages.xz from the signed release, check it, then check package stockfish/Version 17-1/amd64 by Packages.xz. dpkg-deb -x into scratch (no system installation). The binary identifies itself as Stockfish 17.1. Export its embedded NNUEs using export_net; use the explicit resolved network paths and verify both by SHA256 and by the engine's first-search network messages. Set Threads=1, Hash=16 MB, MultiPV=1, Ponder=false, Skill Level=20, UCI_LimitStrength=false, UCI_ShowWDL=true, UCI_Chess960=false, SyzygyProbeLimit=0. No external tablebase or book for these searches.

Before each move, independently send ucinewgame, isready (wait readyok), position startpos moves FULL_HIST, go nodes N searchmoves MOVE (candidate alone); wait for bestmove and require it and the PV head to equal the legal forced move. The primary score is the last reported PV WDL, in the root side-to-move perspective; delta=((2*W8+D8)-(2*W2+D2))/2000. Unbounded-only sensitivity uses the most recent unbounded score from the very same search. Keep flags for last score bounds, mate, both outcomes saturated, and anomaly or missing WDL. Confirm forced mate-in-one known to win for white and black, same move repeated around an intervening different search, plus a fresh process reversing the two real first-dispute evaluations. Do not compare centipawns across engines.

For each scope form one record per each of the 384 game IDs containing row count, delta sum and positive/equal/negative counts. Sample 384 games with replacement, sum their numerators and separately sum their denominators, recompute difference; NumPy default_rng(202611230747), 20,000 bootstrap replicates, percentile 2.5/97.5. Show both all eligible rows and disagreements alone, and the same 382 eligible/98 disputed subset at both budgets. Enhancement claim needs primary lower endpoint strictly positive AND deeper subset same sign, while the official independent reference is still an evaluation model and the search is root-restricted. The previous Syzygy and future equal-paid training match retain their respective objective and adoption roles.

When the entire matrix is present, compare the stored 500k game 2, k=1 pair with its independent sentinel. Rerun the last eligible, disputed k=3 pair in a new Stockfish process (the last in the frozen ordering, irrespective of scores) at 2,000,000 nodes and reverse its production order. Compare best move, depth, node count, score kind/value/bound, published WDL and last unbounded depth/WDL. Verify SQLite integrity, eligibility, chosen-rows repetition, reported legality, calibration sums and complete score coverage. Score or order differences in these controls are disclosed before interpreting the estimate.


The signed Debian stockfish_17-1_amd64.deb SHA256 is 05a3fc698931f7b8c5faf8cdf3e2212e2bb971ce35d468085570d6f025e57ea1; ELF SHA256 38faa5883b03652f847a87ed168b1bfee81b361db9584dae59a51cb91e69d9d6. Query UCI; binary identifies as 17.1. net SHA256 1c0000000000a67d629999d932d0c373f7450ce43cd12d0562868f4eaf9ae2ad and 37f18f62d772f3107e1d6aaca3898c130c3c86f2ab63e6555fbbca20635a899d. Manifest SHA256 536737678048f50cf444308fc381e289fceb52cc206000b8a3081114787cbb6a. Raw CSV uses one zero-difference row for agreed moves and one searched row per forced move for disagreements, at each budget. Preserve the final newline for the digest.
